{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_012SdRnMRWFzmf5QyH7ksUw4","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013JND2hKk2XjKtfUF3Mwn2K","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"source /root/miniconda3/bin/activate && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python << 'PYEOF'\nfrom transformers import AutoConfig, AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\nfrom fla.models.gated_deltanet import GatedDeltaNetConfig, GatedDeltaNetForCausalLM\nAutoConfig.register(\"gated_deltanet\", GatedDeltaNetConfig)\nAutoModelForCausalLM.register(GatedDeltaNetConfig, GatedDeltaNetForCausalLM)\n\nimport torch\npath = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models_1.3b/1.3B-100B-GatedDeltaNet-pure\"\nconfig = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(path)\nprint(f\"use_beta={config.use_beta}, allow_neg_eigval={config.allow_neg_eigval}\")\nconfig.allow_neg_eigval = True\nprint(f\"after fix: allow_neg_eigval={config.allow_neg_eigval}\")\n\nmodel = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(path, config=config, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16)\nmodel = model.to(\"cuda:0\").eval()\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(path)\n\n# Test generation\ninput_ids = tokenizer(\"The capital of France is\", return_tensors=\"pt\").input_ids.to(\"cuda:0\")\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    outputs = model(input_ids)\n    logits = outputs.logits\n    print(f\"logits shape: {logits.shape}\")\n
